namespace = dau_choice

dau_choice.1000 = {
    type = character_event
    title = dau_choice.1000.t
    desc = dau_choice.1000.desc
    theme = stewardship
    after = { dau_choice_after_effect = yes }
    option = {
        name = dau_choice_later
        hidden_effect = {
            if = { limit = { domicile ?= { this = scope:dau_choice_home } }
                domicile = { set_variable = { name = dau_defer_branch_east_asian_estate_garden_03 value = 1 days = 365 } }
            }
        }
        ai_chance = { base = 100 }
    }
    option = {
        name = dau_choice_build_east_asian_estate_garden_leisure_04
        trigger = { dau_choice_context_trigger = yes dau_choice_ready_east_asian_estate_garden_leisure_04 = yes }
        show_as_unavailable = { dau_choice_context_trigger = yes dau_eligible_east_asian_estate_garden_leisure_04 = yes }
        custom_tooltip = dau_choice_cost_east_asian_estate_garden_leisure_04
        hidden_effect = { set_variable = { name = dau_choice_selected value = flag:east_asian_estate_garden_leisure_04 } }
        ai_chance = { base = 0 }
    }
    option = {
        name = dau_choice_build_east_asian_estate_garden_fruit_04
        trigger = { dau_choice_context_trigger = yes dau_choice_ready_east_asian_estate_garden_fruit_04 = yes }
        show_as_unavailable = { dau_choice_context_trigger = yes dau_eligible_east_asian_estate_garden_fruit_04 = yes }
        custom_tooltip = dau_choice_cost_east_asian_estate_garden_fruit_04
        hidden_effect = { set_variable = { name = dau_choice_selected value = flag:east_asian_estate_garden_fruit_04 } }
        ai_chance = { base = 0 }
    }
}

    title = dau_choice.1027.t
    desc = dau_choice.1027.desc
    theme = stewardship
    after = { dau_choice_after_effect = yes }
    option = {
        name = dau_choice_later
        hidden_effect = {
            if = { limit = { domicile ?= { this = scope:dau_choice_home } }
                domicile = { set_variable = { name = dau_defer_external_yurt value = 1 days = 365 } }
            }
        }
        ai_chance = { base = 100 }
    }
    option = {
        name = dau_choice_build_herd_welfare_yurt_01
        trigger = { dau_choice_context_trigger = yes dau_choice_ready_herd_welfare_yurt_01 = yes }
        show_as_unavailable = { dau_choice_context_trigger = yes dau_eligible_herd_welfare_yurt_01 = yes }
        custom_tooltip = dau_choice_cost_herd_welfare_yurt_01
        hidden_effect = { set_variable = { name = dau_choice_selected value = flag:herd_welfare_yurt_01 } }
        ai_chance = { base = 0 }
    }
    option = {
        name = dau_choice_build_character_warfare_yurt_01
        trigger = { dau_choice_context_trigger = yes dau_choice_ready_character_warfare_yurt_01 = yes }
        show_as_unavailable = { dau_choice_context_trigger = yes dau_eligible_character_warfare_yurt_01 = yes }
        custom_tooltip = dau_choice_cost_character_warfare_yurt_01
        hidden_effect = { set_variable = { name = dau_choice_selected value = flag:character_warfare_yurt_01 } }
        ai_chance = { base = 0 }
    }
    option = {
        name = dau_choice_build_mass_warfare_yurt_01
        trigger = { dau_choice_context_trigger = yes dau_choice_ready_mass_warfare_yurt_01 = yes }
        show_as_unavailable = { dau_choice_context_trigger = yes dau_eligible_mass_warfare_yurt_01 = yes }
        custom_tooltip = dau_choice_cost_mass_warfare_yurt_01
        hidden_effect = { set_variable = { name = dau_choice_selected value = flag:mass_warfare_yurt_01 } }
        ai_chance = { base = 0 }
    }
    option = {
        name = dau_choice_build_court_yurt_01
        trigger = { dau_choice_context_trigger = yes dau_choice_ready_court_yurt_01 = yes }
        show_as_unavailable = { dau_choice_context_trigger = yes dau_eligible_court_yurt_01 = yes }
        custom_tooltip = dau_choice_cost_court_yurt_01
        hidden_effect = { set_variable = { name = dau_choice_selected value = flag:court_yurt_01 } }
        ai_chance = { base = 0 }
    }
    option = {

        hidden_effect = { set_variable = { name = dau_choice_selected value = flag:family_yurt_01 } }
        ai_chance = { base = 0 }
    }
    option = {
        name = dau_choice_next
        trigger = { dau_choice_context_trigger = yes dau_page_external_yurt_1_trigger = yes }
        hidden_effect = { set_variable = dau_choice_navigating if = { limit = { dau_page_external_yurt_1_trigger = yes } trigger_event = { id = dau_choice.1028 } } }
